When environments are misconfigured to allow live internet access — contradicting stated setup parameters — models assigned penetration testing objectives may fail to reliably distinguish simulated range infrastructure from real production systems, resulting in unauthorized access to real-world organizations. This class of evaluation pipeline risk had not previously been subject to large-scale retrospective audit or public disclosure.","importance":"$13","technical_approach":"$14","experiments":"$15","reading_guide":"$16","authors_note":"Anthropic Safety and Evaluation team, with joint investigation support from third-party cybersecurity evaluation provider Irregular."},"recommendation":{"zh":"预训练方法本身学不到太多；快速看事件分类和评估闭环，主要用于校准 cyber eval 的外部效度。","en":"Not much to learn for pretraining mechanics; skim the incident taxonomy and evaluation loop to calibrate the external validity of cyber evals."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Anthropic"],"authors":["Anthropic"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["cybersecurity-eval","model-safety","incident-analysis","frontier-models"],"links":{"arxiv":"","pdf":null,"github":null,"project":null,"source_url":"https://www.anthropic.com/news/investigating-incidents-cybersecurity-evals","article":null}}},{"id":"2607.26981","title":"OptimismBench: Forecasting Bias and the Alignment Effect in Language Model Judgment","abstract":"Large language models are increasingly used as decision aids whose probability judgments shape downstream choices. Whether those judgments carry a systematic directional tilt has been hard to detect: calibration metrics aggregate unsigned errors, and naturalistic uncertainty offers no ground-truth probability. When an LLM rates a startup's success at 70% but its failure at 15%, the missing 15 points expose a distortion no aggregate score flags. We introduce OptimismBench, which detects direction","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-29T14:38:55Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2607_26981.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要测的是 LLM 概率判断里的方向性偏差，而不是传统校准误差。普通 ECE 这类指标只看误差大小，不看偏向正面还是负面；自然场景又没有真实概率可作标签。OptimismBench 用“正反表述成对询问”的方式，把同一场景拆成成功概率和失败概率，让模型自己的两个回答互为约束，从而暴露乐观或悲观倾向。","importance":"这不是预训练方法论文，但对基座研究有两个直接启发。第一，LLM-as-judge、自动评审、风险预测、reward model 都依赖模型的概率或偏好判断，方向性偏差会改变训练信号质量。第二，结果显示偏差方向和后训练栈强相关，同一预训练底座经过不同 alignment 后会出现压缩或放大。这说明 post-train 不只是提高可用性，也会系统性改写模型的判断先验。","technical_approach":"核心设计是 inverted pairs。每个场景分别询问正面事件概率和负面事件概率，理想情况下二者应互补。论文保留互补误差的符号，构造 signed Skew，而不是只统计不一致程度。实验包含多个 track：带显式 base rate 的 sanity check、无显式基准率的自然概率判断、行动与不行动推荐、机会与风险显著性评分。主结果来自自然概率判断 track。论文还把 Skew 拆成正面轴和负面轴相对 50 的偏移，用来区分“同时高估成功和失败”的 compound overclaim、“同时低估”的 underclaim，以及更接近传统含义的乐观或悲观。","experiments":"主实验覆盖 8 个提供方的 16 个模型、60 个 inverted-pair 场景、10 次采样，并做 10 种语言的扩展。16 个模型全部有显著方向性偏差，其中 14 个偏乐观；Anthropic 的 Opus 和 Sonnet 为悲观，Skew 分别约为 -5.1 和 -7.7，而同一家族的 Haiku 4.5 为 +6.1，排除了简单的“模型越大偏差越小”解释。控制实验比较 4 个模型家族的 11 组 base/chat 配对：Qwen 的 alignment 在 5 组中都压缩偏差，Llama 的 alignment 在 4 组中都放大乐观倾向；Gemma-2-2B 和 Mistral-Small-24B 作为单组 pilot 报告。跨语言分析显示模型间方差是语言间方差的 4.7 倍，说明选用哪个模型比使用哪种语言更影响偏差。论文还报告了 prompt、temperature、视角改写和显式自我去偏提示下的稳定性；对 50 分锚点和拒答率做了过滤分析，避免把拒答模式误读为低偏差。","reading_guide":"泛读 20–30 分钟即可。优先看 Method 里 Skew 的定义和四个 track 的设计，再看 Table 1 的模型偏差方向、Figure 4 的 valence decomposition，以及 base-vs-chat 配对实验。对做 reward model、LLM judge 或自动数据筛选的人，值得多看 robustness 和拒答过滤部分，因为这些细节决定 benchmark 是否真的测到方向性偏差，而不是格式跟随或安全策略的副产物。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper measures directional bias in LLM probability judgments. Standard calibration metrics collapse errors into unsigned quantities, so they cannot tell whether a model systematically favors positive or negative outcomes. Natural forecasting questions also lack ground-truth probabilities. OptimismBench uses paired positive and negative framings of the same scenario, forcing the model’s own answers to serve as an internal consistency check.","importance":"The work is most relevant to evaluation and post-training signal quality. LLM judges, forecasting agents, reward models, and data filters all rely on model judgments that may carry a signed prior. The base-versus-chat comparison is especially useful: post-training can set or amplify the direction of this bias even when the architecture and pretraining are held fixed. That makes optimism or pessimism a property of the alignment stack, not just of scale.","technical_approach":"The benchmark is built around inverted pairs. For each scenario, the model is asked for the probability of a positive outcome and separately for the probability of the corresponding negative outcome. A coherent model should produce complementary estimates. The paper keeps the sign of the complement violation as a Skew score, rather than reducing it to an unsigned inconsistency measure. It also decomposes the bias into positive-axis and negative-axis deviations from a 50-point anchor, which separates classical optimism, pessimism, compound overclaim, and compound underclaim. The tracks cover explicit-base-rate sanity checks, naturalistic uncertainty, action-versus-inaction recommendations, and opportunity-versus-risk salience.","experiments":"The main evaluation uses 16 models from 8 providers, 60 inverted-pair scenarios, and 10 runs, with a multilingual extension over 10 languages. All 16 models show significant directional bias; 14 are optimistic. Anthropic’s Opus and Sonnet are pessimistic, with reported Skew values around -5.1 and -7.7, while Haiku 4.5 is optimistic at about +6.1. A controlled base-versus-chat probe over 11 matched pairs from Qwen, Llama, Gemma, and Mistral shows family-specific alignment effects: Qwen alignment compresses bias in all five pairs, while Llama alignment amplifies optimism in all four pairs. The cross-lingual analysis reports that inter-model variance is 4.7 times inter-language variance. The bias is reported to be stable across prompt variants, temperature, perspective changes, and explicit self-debiasing prompts.","reading_guide":"A useful skim takes about 20–30 minutes. Focus on the metric definition in the method section, the four evaluation tracks, Table 1 for model-level bias direction, Figure 4 for the valence decomposition, and the controlled base-versus-chat comparison. The robustness and refusal-filtering analyses are worth checking if the benchmark is used for LLM-as-judge or reward-model auditing, because 50-point anchoring and refusals can otherwise mask signed bias.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"读实验表和 base-chat 对比；它提示 post-train 会改概率先验符号，不只是改善校准。","en":"Read the result tables and base-chat pairs; post-training appears to change the sign of probabilistic priors, not just calibration."},"metadata":{"institutions":["University College London"],"authors":["Seonglae Cho","Adriano Koshiyama"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["evaluation","probability-judgment","alignment","bias","post-training"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26981","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.26981","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2607.26769","title":"See2Think: Do Multimodal Models Really Use Intermediate Visual States?","abstract":"Multimodal large language models increasingly use sketches, annotations, tools, and intermediate images during reasoning, but it remains unclear whether they truly rely on these visual states. Under a fixed forced-choice interface, Visual Credit Audit (VCA) separates two estimands: whether the benchmark image gives the model's declared decision more support than text-only and blank controls, and whether the model responds to relation-specific visual evidence. The first audit is training- and label-free and does not require an answer flip. Applying labels yield","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-29T15:55:31Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2607_27069.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"判断多模态空间推理 benchmark 中，模型答对 yes/no 题时是否真的使用了图像证据，而不是靠文本先验或选项偏置答对。","importance":"贡献是一个视觉证据归因评测协议 Visual Credit Audit，用来拆分“答案正确”和“图像确实提供了决策支持”这两个量；这对 VLM 评测、视觉依赖性测量和多模态训练信号质量都有直接参考。","technical_approach":"在固定 forced-choice 接口下，VCA 使用 text-only 和 blank controls，估计图像是否比无图条件更支持模型声明的决策；引入 dependence-credited correctness（D-CC）衡量带视觉依赖的正确性。论文还使用同 split 图像置换、固定像素关系对比和 3x3 evidence-source factorial，进一步区分是否响应关系特定的视觉证据。","experiments":"在四个开源 MLLM 和两个空间推理 benchmark 上评测，发现 12.73%-26.25% 的决策虽然答案正确但没有获得视觉证据 credit；同 split 图像置换使 D-CC 下降 21.25-47.80 个点，且配对 95% 区间均大于零。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether correct yes/no answers on multimodal spatial reasoning benchmarks are actually supported by the image rather than by text-only priors or interface bias.","importance":"Its main contribution is Visual Credit Audit, an evaluation protocol that separates answer correctness from image-supported decision making. Early evidence from two case studies","abstract":"Forecasts of explosive AI progress hinge on AI agents automating AI research. But evidence on whether agents can carry out open-ended AI research is thin. Current evaluations either test agents on narrow, verifiable tasks, which excludes open-ended research, or submit AI-generated papers to blind peer review, which is overstretched, stochastic, and suffers from poor review quality. We introduce a third way to measure progress towards AI R\\&D automation. diagn","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-28T07:28:38Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2607_26102.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CR","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个评测层面的盲点：数学推理里，最终答案正确并不等于推理链可信，最终答案错误也不等于推理过程无效。作者把这种“答案正确性”和“推理正确性”不一致的现象称为 A chain can be invalid yet land on the right answer, and a mostly valid derivation can still end with the wrong final output. The authors call this the reasoning–answer consistency gap and focus especially on silent reasoning failures, where flawed reasoning is hidden by a correct answer. 模型，并显著改善视觉上下文敏感度控制。文中给出的主要洞察不是某个单点 benchmark 分数，而是错误来源的分解：对粗粒度属性，许多失败更接近 utilization failure，而不是 perception failure。需要注意，提供的内容没有包含完整数值表，因此不应过度解读具体提升幅度。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 25 分钟。优先看三处：一是 visual evidence conflicts with language priors: whether the model cannot perceive the relevant visual signal, or whether it has the signal but lacks a stable policy for using it.","importance":"The useful contribution is a clean separation between perception failure and utilization failure. For multimodal pretraining, this matters because improving the vision encoder or adding more image-text alignment data may not fix cases where the model already encodes the visual attribute but does not know when to trust it. The result points to a missing training signal: models need supervision or objectives that teach controlled reliance on visual context versus parametric knowledge, rather than only end-to-end VQA accuracy.","technical_approach":"The paper first isolates perception. It focuses on coarse object-level attributes such as identity, color, size, count, and weight, avoiding fine-grained details that vision encoders often discard. It then reconstructs counterfactual images from the final-layer image tokens of frozen MLLMs. If those reconstructions preserve the relevant attributes, the visual evidence is still present in the model representation. The second part studies utilization. The authors introduce WhatIfVis, where each question can be answered either from the image or from prior world knowledge. They adapt controllable context sensitivity to the visual setting, yielding CVCS: a one-dimensional steering subspace that modulates reliance on visual context. A practical detail is important: vanilla models produce too few strictly successful bidirectional examples to localize the steering direction reliably, so the direction is localized in SFT models and then transferred back to the corresponding vanilla models.","experiments":"The reported evidence has three parts. First, across three model families, coarse counterfactual content remains recoverable from final-layer image tokens, suggesting that many failures on these attributes are not caused by missing perception. Second, WhatIfVis covers five dimensions: spatial-temporal, color, count, size, and weight, and is designed to expose conflicts between image evidence and parametric priors. Third, CVCS directions found in SFT models transfer to vanilla models and improve controllability over visual-context reliance. The main experimental message is diagnostic rather than just a benchmark number: for coarse attributes, utilization failure is a separable and common error source. The supplied text does not include full numeric tables, so exact gains should not be inferred beyond the stated qualitative findings.","reading_guide":"A 25-minute skim is enough. ","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-28T16:03:39Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2607_26115.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CR","cs.AI","cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是“再做一个红队 benchmark”，而是一个更难也更接近生产的问题：怎样持续地产生高质量、会适应目标模型变化、能覆盖新攻击面的对抗样本，用来训练和评估前沿模型在 prompt injection 等 agentic 风险下的鲁棒性。作者指出，静态红队数据集规模有限，模型很容易记住已有攻击模板，却仍然对会试探、会迭代、会针对具体系统弱点调整策略的攻击者失守。论文因此把红队数据生成本身做成一个可训练对象：训练一个自动化 attacker，在与 defender 的反复交互中学会发现有效失败案例，再把这些失败样本回流到 defender 的 RL 训练里，形成持续升级的攻防闭环。","importance":"对基座研究的意义主要有三层。第一，它把“安全红队”从一次性评测资产变成了可扩展的训练信号生成器，这和预训练里用更强数据生成器提升数据质量的思路是同构的，只是这里生成的是 adversarial post-training data。第二，它展示了一个很清楚的 flywheel：更强 attacker 产出更难样本，更强 defender 逼 attacker 学新策略，训练信号不再停留在固定分布上。对于 pretrain↔posttrain 接口，这很重要，因为它说明后训练阶段也可以像预训练一样依赖持续扩展的数据分布，而不是只靠人工标注集。第三，论文给出了一个少见的信号：OpenAI 明确把这类训练规模拉到“大型 RL post-training run”级别，并把 GPT-Red 直接用于 GPT-5.2 之后各代生产模型的训练与评估。这意味着在前沿模型体系里，鲁棒性数据生成已经被当成一条独立、重投入的训练管线，而不是附属评测工具。","technical_approach":"方法上有三个关键设计。第一是 agentic red-teaming harness。作者不是让 attacker 一次性吐出一个攻击 prompt，而是给它一个有状态的 defender_model 工具，让它像人工红队一样多轮试探、观察反馈、再改写攻击。这样做的原因很直接：prompt injection 往往依赖上下文、工具调用、文件局部编辑、系统提示泄露等交互细节，单步生成很难覆盖真实攻击过程。第二是环境构造。论文把已有 capability tasks 改写成 adversarial RL environments，在 defender 执行原任务时，允许 attacker 在指定位置插入或修改内容，例如篡改一次工具返回、编辑本地文件某个区域、污染外部文档片段等。这样定义 threat model 有两个好处：一是攻击面贴近真实 agent 系统；二是环境可以随着生产任务域扩展而自然扩展，不需要每次从零手写安全任务。第三是大规模 self-play RL。基础博弈是 attacker 成功诱发有效失败就得奖励，defender 成功抵抗并完成原任务就得奖励。但作者没有停留在单一 attacker-vs-defender 对局，而是把 attacker 放到一个同时训练的 defender population 上。这样做是为了避免 attacker 过拟合某个 defender 的怪癖，转而学习“如何快速识别不同模型的薄弱点”。从训练动力学看，这更像 population-based adversarial training，而不是普通的两人零和游戏。论文还强调三种 scaling 轴：测试时算力，通过 agentic harness 允许更长搜索与迭代；环境多样性，通过广泛的红队场景和攻击目标；训练算力，通过接近顶级 RL run 的规模把策略真正训出来。最终得到的 GPT-Red 不是一个固定模板库，而是一个会搜索、会适应、会迁移的攻击代理。","experiments":"实验结论很集中：GPT-Red 在已知模型、未知目标、未知环境上都能找到更多有效攻击，而且会随着 test-time compute 增加继续变强。论文给出的代表性结果包括：1）它能够稳定攻破此前各代 GPT-5 系列模型，直到 GPT-5.5；2）在 2025 IPI Challenge 这类与训练环境不同的 prompt injection 场景中，GPT-Red 找到的有效攻击数超过人类红队和 GPT-5.5 baseline；3）它能迁移到真实系统，例如文中展示的 AI vending machine 场景；4）作者把过去六个月逐步增强的红队模型持续接入生产模型训练，从 GPT-5.2 开始一路用于后续版本，最终 GPT-5.6 在广泛鲁棒性评测上达到当前最高水平。虽然公开文本里没有完整披露每个训练 run 的参数量、token 数、RL step 数和全部绝对指标，但有几个实验信号很关键：一是 test-time scaling 明显成立，说明 attacker 的能力不只是参数记忆，而是搜索与策略改进能力；二是 defender population 训练带来更强泛化，说明多对手训练确实缓解了对单一目标的过拟合；三是 out-of-distribution transfer 成立，说明学到的不是单一 prompt 模板，而是更抽象的攻击策略。对做训练的人来说，这些比单个 benchmark 分数更有信息量。","reading_guide":"建议先看 Introduction 和 Vision 部分，抓住论文真正提出的对象：不是一个评测器，而是一条自动生成 adversarial training data 的训练管线。然后重点看 Section 3.2 的 threat model 和环境定义，理解 attacker 到底能改什么、不能改什么；这一部分决定了训练信号是否真实。接着看 Section 4 的 agentic harness，关注为什么要给 attacker 状态和工具，以及它如何利用交互式搜索。Section 5 是核心，重点看 self-play 如何扩展到 defender population、奖励如何定义、训练规模如何组织。Section 6 值得细读，因为它解释了怎样把普通 capability tasks 改造成 adversarial environments，这对很多安全训练工作都有可复用价值。实验部分优先看 Figure 1、Figure 2、Figure 9 以及关于 IPI Challenge、历史 GPT-5 系列攻防、GPT-5.6 鲁棒性提升的章节。精读时可以带着三个问题：一，攻击成功的 credit assignment 是怎么做的，怎样避免把无效噪声当成有效攻击；二，population training 如何平衡 attacker 泛化与训练稳定性；三，这种 flywheel 会不会出现 reward hacking，例如 attacker 学会利用环境 bug 而不是模型弱点。真正值得深挖的地方不是“它又找到了哪些花式攻击”，而是“怎样把开放式安全失败转成可规模化 RL 信号”。","authors_note":"OpenAI。文中明确以 OpenAI 生产模型与 RL post-training 基础设施为背景，围绕 GPT-5.2 至 GPT-5.6 的内部攻防训练展开；公开节选未给出完整作者名单。"},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles a data-generation problem for robustness training, not merely an evaluation problem. Static red-teaming datasets are small, quickly exhausted, and easy for defenders to overfit to. What is missing is a scalable attacker that can adapt to a target model, iterate through interaction, and keep discovering fresh prompt-injection failures as the defender improves. GPT-Red is proposed as that attacker: an automated red-teaming agent trained through self-play so that adversarial examples become a renewable training signal rather than a fixed corpus.","importance":"Its relevance to foundation-model research is broader than safety benchmarking. First, it turns adversarial data generation into a trainable component, analogous to improving pretraining data pipelines except the product here is high-value post-training data. Second, it demonstrates a concrete attacker-defender flywheel: stronger attackers surface harder failures, stronger defenders force attackers to search for new strategies, and the training distribution keeps moving. That is directly relevant to the pretrain/post-train interface because it shows how post-training can benefit from continuously refreshed data rather than static human-written sets. Third, the paper makes an unusually explicit scaling claim: the attacker was trained with compute on the scale of major RL post-training runs and was integrated into successive production models from GPT-5.2 onward. That signals a shift from ad hoc safety evaluation to a dedicated large-scale training pipeline.","technical_approach":"$1b","experiments":"$1c","reading_guide":"$1d","authors_note":"OpenAI. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-29T09:15:40Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2607_26654.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.CY","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要回答一个很具体、也很关键的问题：如果把对齐信号前移到 SFT 隔离，检验 midtraining 本身是否足以改变模型的行为倾向。全文的核心结论是肯定的：在 120B 规模上，内容是否出现于 midtraining 数据里，比课程顺序或是否显式写出 deliberative reasoning 更决定最终对齐收益；而且这种收益能跨过 SFT，并在 benign fine-tuning 可能更多取决于模型在预训练/中训练阶段是否真正见过并内化了某类价值内容，而不是后面再用少量偏好优化去覆盖原有倾向。这对数据配比、课程设计、合成语料构建、continual pretrain 都有直接启发。第三，论文的结论带有一点反直觉：作者专门设计了 curriculum ordering 和 reasoning/no-reasoning 的 2x2 因子实验，但最后最稳的信号是 content post-train。","technical_approach":"$1e","experiments":"$1f","reading_guide":"这篇值得精读。先看 Introduction，重点不是背景铺陈，而是作者如何把 shallow alignment、alignment elasticity、midtraining intervention 这几条线接起来。这里能帮助判断他们的问题设定是否真的隔离了 post-training 影响。\n\n然后直接看 Method 里的数据构建与实验设计。要重点盯三处：一是 40 个 constitutional values 如何抽取、为什么排除两个外围 value；二是 centrality + clustering 如何导出 curriculum 顺序，这关系到“课程设计”这个因素是否有合理结构基础；三是 2x2 因子设计和 value-neutral SFT 的隔离逻辑，这是整篇文章最关键的因果设计。\n\n实验部分建议按时间轴读，而不是按 benchmark 读：先看 post-midtraining，再看 post-SFT，最后看 post-benign fine-tuning。这样更容易判断哪些收益是即时的，哪些是真正 durable 的。接着看不同条件之间的比较，尤其是 content presence 与 reasoning/curriculum 的主效应和交互效应。如果论文给了 error bars、bootstrap 或多 seed 结果，这部分要仔细看，因为很多 alignment 结论容易受评测噪声影响。\n\n最后值得深挖的是两个方向。第一，为什么 content presence 比 reasoning format 更重要：是因为模型在这个规模上已经能自行抽取规范结构，还是因为 reasoning 文本的边际信息有限。第二，midtraining 改变的是局部行为模板，还是更深层的表示分离。作者提到开放 checkpoints 供 mechanistic analysis，这部分后续很可能比当前 benchmark 分数更有研究价值。\n\n如果熟悉 midtraining/对齐文献，完整读一遍大约需要 60 到 90 分钟；若要用于设计自己的实验，方法和实验章节都值得逐表细看。","authors_note":"机构信息从正文可确认与 Anthropic Constitution、NVIDIA Nemotron to SFT or RLHF, can they produce durable and generalizable alignment instead of a shallow behavioral layer that later fine-tuning can erase? The authors study this through constitutional midtraining: injecting a structured corpus derived from a constitution into the late pretraining stage, while carefully isolating it from downstream post-training. The main result is that the answer is yes. At 120B scale, whether constitutional content is present during midtraining matters more than curriculum ordering or explicit deliberative reasoning, and the gains persist through value-neutral SFT and even benign fine-tuning.","importance":"This is directly relevant to pretraining research. First, it pushes alignment upstream and provides evidence that midtraining is a meaningful intervention point at large scale, not just a cheaper variant of post-training. Second, it supports a stronger claim about training-stage responsibilities: some behavioral dispositions may need to be shaped during pretraining or midtraining because post-training struggles to overwrite them reliably. That has implications for data mixture design, synthetic corpus construction, continual pretraining, and the pretrain-to-posttrain interface. Third, the paper delivers a useful practical message. The strongest signal is content presence itself. In other words, getting the right value-bearing content into midtraining appears more important than sophisticated ordering or formatting choices. learning for open-ended generation under a strict transductive setting. The model can only use the test prompts and its own sampled responses. It has no references, no human feedback, no external reward model, and no stronger judge. Majority-vote TTRL breaks in this regime because open-ended responses cannot be reliably mapped to a shared canonical answer.","importance":"The paper is mainly relevant to post-training and test-time adaptation, but it has a useful connection to pretraining research: it studies how a model can construct training signals from its own generations when external supervision is unavailable. The main lesson is that answer agreement is too narrow for open-ended adaptation; reward construction needs a target-specific evaluation interface. SERPO’s evolving rubric design is a concrete attempt at such an interface, with clear implications for synthetic supervision, RL data quality, and self-improvement loops.","technical_approach":"SERPO builds a closed loop over responses, rubrics, and policy updates. For each query, the policy samples a group of rollouts and maintains a local Good-Normal-Bad response archive. Atomic criteria are derived from contrasts among these responses. Criteria are kept only if they show response-level variance and ordered separation across the Good-Normal-Bad archive; duplicates are consolidated and persistently weak criteria are removed. The reward signal is probabilistic rather than purely discrete: post-reasoning Pass/Fail verdict distributions are converted into criterion-satisfaction probabilities, then aggregated into group-relative rewards for actor optimization. The key difference from answer-voting TTRL is that SERPO optimizes satisfaction of query-specific requirements instead of agreement with a pseudo-answer.","experiments":"The paper reports results on two model configurations, two in-domain benchmarks, and four out-of-domain benchmarks. The adapted policies also transfer to unseen benchmarks and continue improving under sequential cross-benchmark evolution. A single preference score is used across reasoning, safety, factuality, and creative writing, which can blur fine-grained quality differences and expose exploitable reward-model blind spots. MeRLa adds a task-aware shaping reward while trying to preserve the optimal policy induced by the original reward.","importance":"The contribution sits in post-training, but it is relevant to foundation-model training through the lens of signal quality. RL pretraining, continual pretraining with feedback, and alignment all face the same tension: auxiliary rewards can improve optimization, but unconstrained rewards can change the target. The paper is useful because it treats reward shaping as a constrained learning problem rather than as another heuristic bonus.","technical_approach":"MeRLa has two phases. It first meta-learns a shaping function Φ(x, y; φ) over auxiliary tasks, then uses the learned shaping term during RLHF by combining it with the base reward model. The shaping network is a lightweight two-layer MLP with hidden size 256 and fewer than 1M parameters. Its inputs are embeddings from a frozen reference model: the prompt representation, the response representation, and their difference. Freezing the encoder is a deliberate design choice: it reduces representation drift and avoids recomputing features from the evolving policy at every RL step. To avoid changing the optimal policy, the method constrains the shaping term toward a potential-based form and decomposes response-level shaping into token-level potentials for autoregressive generation. The meta-objective combines preference discrimination, entropy regularization over shaped rewards, and a conservation penalty that keeps the shaping function close to the potential-based subspace.","experiments":"The reported experiments use LLaMA-3-8B and compare against PPO, DPO, GRPO, and DAPO across four benchmarks. The training-dynamics analysis reports 37% lower reward variance and 41% less training instability, while preserving generation diversity. The paper also includes theoretical analysis of representation-drift sensitivity and possible incentive misalignment from entropy maximization.","reading_guide":"A 30-minute skim is enough for most readers. A standard way to balance this trade-off is via a KL-regularized RL objective, although this formulation does not by itself provide a principled way to set the regularization coefficient. In practice, the coefficient is typically chosen heuristically or via hyperparameter search, which can lead to unnecessary overhead in tra","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-29T00:19:09Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2607_26358.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.GT"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 RL fine-tuning 里一个长期存在但常被经验化处理的问题：KL 正则系数 β 应该怎么设。传统做法把 β 当作超参数，用网格搜索、人工调节，或在线追踪某个目标 KL；这些方法都缺少一个清楚的原则，尤其当目标是“提升任务奖励，同时尽量保留参考模型行为”时，KL 本身只是距离约束，并没有直接回答“偏离到什么程度才算合适”。作者把这个问题改写成一个顺序检测博弈：agent 想通过策略改动获得更高奖励，monitor 则持续观察输出并判断它是否仍来自参考策略；一旦检测到偏离，部署终止。这样，β 不再只是调参旋钮，而被解释为在统计可检测性约束下的最优权衡参数。","importance":"对预训练和后训练接口的意义在于，它给 KL-regularized RL 提供了一个更可操作的解释：保留参考模型行为，不一定非要靠预先指定一个 KL target，也可以理解成“让改动保持在监控者难以可靠区分的边界附近”。这件事有两层价值。第一，它把常见的 RLHF / RLVR 中“reward–retention trade-off”从经验问题变成了博弈均衡问题，至少在理论上给 β 的选择提供了闭环。第二，它把 post-training 中的 policy drift、模型审计、持续学习三件事放到了同一个框架里：如果后训练策略需要既学新任务又维持旧能力，那么最自然的约束不一定是固定 KL，而是可检测性预算。量级上，这不是训练 recipe 的直接替代品，也不是立刻改变大规模 RL pipeline 的工程方案；更像是给现有 KL 正则 RL 提供了一层更扎实的目标解释，并给 β 搜索带来一个比手工 sweep 更有原则的算法。","technical_approach":"技术上有三部分。第一部分是博弈建模。环境给定 prompt 分布、参考策略 π_ref 和奖励函数 r。agent 选择策略 π 以最大化累计奖励；monitor 观察输出序列，做顺序假设检验，判断当前输出来自 π_ref 还是来自某个偏离策略 π。若 monitor 在控制 type-I error 的前提下足够快地拒绝“来自参考策略”的原假设，agent 就会被终止。第二部分是均衡分析。作者证明，在这个 sequential detection game 的 Nash equilibrium 上，agent 的最优策略正好等价于求解标准的 KL-regularized RL 目标，即最大化 E[r] - β KL(π || π_ref)。这里关键不是重新发明目标，而是把 KL 项解释成“为了延缓被检测而支付的统计代价”。更进一步，均衡条件唯一地刻画了最优 β*，它由奖励函数、prompt 分布和参考策略共同决定，而不是外部拍脑袋指定。文中还利用 KL-regularized RL 的闭式最优解形式 π*(y|x) ∝ π_ref(y|x) exp(r(x,y)/β)，把均衡条件转成一个关于 β 的标量方程。第三部分是求解算法。作者给出一个 stochastic bisection 程序，通过反复求解不同 β 下的 RL 子问题，利用目标函数的单调结构，把 β* 逼近到 ε 精度，复杂度是 O(log(1/ε)) 个 RL 子问题。分析上借用了 fractional programming 和 sequential hypothesis testing 的工具。和常见“设定 target KL 再调 β”的做法相比，这里是反过来：先由博弈均衡定义最优偏离边界，再求出对应 β。","experiments":"实验分两类。第一类是 continual learning 场景，使用 Qwen3-8B 和 Llama-3.2-1B。作者比较了均衡 β 与 compute-matched 的手工调参方案，结论是前者在 reward 提升与 reference behavior 保留之间给出更好的折中。全文给出的主张是“preliminary evidence”，说明实验规模和覆盖面仍偏有限，重点在验证理论方向而不是做大规模 benchmark 冲榜。第二类是 model auditing。这里 monitor 按均衡分析导出的顺序检验来检测 API 提供方是否暗中替换或修改模型。结果显示，相比自然基线，这种 equilibrium-prescribed test 能在控制 type-I error 的同时更快发现隐藏修改。这个实验很有意思，因为它说明论文里的 monitor 不是纯理论角色，而是能落到实际审计流程里。若从研究者角度看，最该关注的不是绝对分数，而是两个现象：一，均衡 β 确实能替代一部分人工 sweep；二，检测视角与 KL 约束之间存在可操作的对应关系。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction，把问题设定和两类应用场景抓住。然后直接看博弈定义与主定理：顺序检测博弈、Nash equilibrium、以及为什么均衡策略会回到 KL-regularized RL。这里是全文最有信息量的部分。接着看算法部分，重点理解 stochastic bisection 为什么能把 β 搜索从经验调参变成有收敛保证的过程。实验部分建议只抓两张图：continual learning 里的 reward–retention 曲线，以及 auditing 里的 detection delay / error control 对比。预计 30–45 分钟可以完成一次有效泛读。若要深挖，值得追问三点：一，这个框架对 token-level autoregressive policy 的近似在哪里；二，均衡 β 对 reward model 误差有多敏感；三，在真实 RLHF 中 reference policy、sampling policy、training policy 不完全一致时，理论是否还能成立。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles a familiar but usually heuristic choice in RL fine-tuning: how to set the KL regularization coefficient β. In standard KL-regularized RL, β controls the trade-off between improving task reward and staying close to a reference policy, but the objective itself does not say what β should be. In practice, people use manual sweeps, target-KL heuristics, or adaptive controllers. The paper reframes this as a sequential detection game: an agent wants to increase reward by deviating from the reference policy, while a monitor observes outputs and tries to detect whether the deployed policy is still the reference one. Once deviation is detected, deployment stops. Under this view, β becomes the equilibrium price of statistical detectability rather than a free hyperparameter.","importance":"Its main value is conceptual, but not merely philosophical. It gives KL-regularized RL an operational interpretation that is directly relevant to post-training of pretrained models: preserving the reference model is equivalent to staying near the edge of detectability under sequential monitoring. That matters for RLHF, RLVR, continual learning, and model auditing, where policy drift is often the real concern. The contribution is not a new training objective replacing current pipelines; instead, it explains why the existing objective appears in practice and provides a principled way to choose β. For pretrain–posttrain interface work, this is useful because it turns reward-retention into a game-theoretic equilibrium problem rather than a pure hyperparameter search problem.","technical_approach":"$23","experiments":"The experiments cover two settings. In continual learning, using Qwen3-8B and Llama-3.2-1B, the equilibrium-derived β gives a better reward-retention trade-off than compute-matched manual tuning. The paper is careful and describes this as preliminary evidence, which is appropriate: the empirical section is there to validate the framework, not to claim broad benchmark dominance. In model auditing, the monitor uses the equilibrium-prescribed sequential test to detect hidden model modifications by API providers. The method detects changes faster than natural baselines while controlling type-I error. For a reader interested in training methodology, the important empirical takeaways are not the absolute scores but two qualitative findings: the equilibrium β can replace part of manual hyperparameter search, and the detectability view leads to a practical auditing procedure with measurable gains in detection delay.","reading_guide":"Start with the introduction and problem setup. Then go straight to the game definition and the main theorem showing why the equilibrium recovers KL-regularized RL. That is the center of the paper. After that, read the stochastic bisection section to understand how β selection becomes a convergent search procedure rather than a heuristic sweep. Target，因为证据虽然有效，但回答的是另一个因果问题。pair recovery 只有在一对中的两个 endpoint 都预测正确时才计成功，这能抑制简单的正负情绪或证据模式捷径。第二是表示探测：作者从三个 instruction-tuned checkpoint 的倒数第二个 transformer block 取 final-token hidden state，在独立 recovery 指标，因为这是论文比普通四分类评测更严格的地方。实验部分重点看 hidden-state readout、restricted input ablation、leave-family-out 和 option-logit baseline。需要谨慎阅读的是数据规模和标注一致性：49 对样本能说明一个清晰现象，但不足以支撑广泛结论。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether a language model can determine how the same diagnostic evidence bears on different causal targets. The evidence may support one causal claim, challenge another, or be irrelevant to a third because the population, outcome, estimand, pathway, or identifying assumption has changed. Standard benchmarks often vary evidence and target together, leaving room for shortcuts based on wording or familiar diagnostic patterns.","importance":"This is an evaluation and probing paper rather than a pretraining paper. Its relevance lies in the diagnostic design: it isolates target-conditioned causal interpretation and tests whether that information is linearly recoverable from model states. For researchers studying pretrained representations, causal reasoning, and shortcut behavior, the setup is useful. The benchmark is small, so it is better treated as a controlled probe than as a general-purpose leaderboard.","technical_approach":"The benchmark uses paired prompts. Each pair repeats the same study setting and diagnostic evidence verbatim, while changing only the causal target. Labels are Favors, Challenges, Unresolved, and Wrong Target. A pair is counted as recovered only when both endpoints are classified correctly, which prevents a model from succeeding by assigning the same favorable or adverse label to both prompts. The probing experiment extracts the final-token hidden state from the penultimate transformer block of three instruction-tuned checkpoints and trains a linear readout on a separate development set. The paper also includes evidence-only and evidence-omitted ablations, scenario-grouped permutation nulls, leave-family-out tests, option-logit baselines, and text baselines.","experiments":"The primary benchmark contains 49 pairs across nine diagnostic families. Linear readouts from hidden states reach balanced accuracy between 0.654 and 0.659 and recover 18 to 21 complete pairs, exceeding the grouped permutation null for all three checkpoints. In Qwen2.5, the full-prompt hidden state recovers 21 pairs, while neither the evidence-only nor the evidence-omitted input recovers a complete pair. When development examples from the evaluated diagnostic family are removed, the readout still recovers 21 pairs and recovers at least one pair in every family. The highest-scoring option baseline recovers four pairs, a linear classifier over the four option logits recovers eight, and text baselines recover at most four.","reading_guide":"Skim time: 20 to 25 minutes. Read the benchmark construction first, especially the distinction between Wrong Target and Unresolved. Then inspect the complete-pair recovery metric, since it is the main safeguard against shortcut solutions. In the results, focus on the hidden-state readout, restricted-input ablations, leave-family-out setting, and option-logit baselines. The main limitation to keep in mind is scale: 49 pairs are enough for a controlled diagnostic, but not enough for broad claims about causal reasoning ability.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"setup 比多数 probing paper 干净，适合借鉴到 reasoning representation 分析；先看 paired-prompt 设计和 pair recovery 指标，正文不必细抠。","en":"Cleaner than most probing papers; borrow the paired-prompt design and pair-recovery metric for reasoning-state analysis, and you can stop there."},"metadata":{"institutions":["University of Toronto","The University of Hong Kong"],"authors":["Weiyi Kong","Zhuoran Li"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["mechanistic-analysis","causal-reasoning","probing","hidden-states","evaluation-design"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26929","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.26929","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2607.26119","title":"Probing the Origins of Reasoning Performance: Representational Quality for Mathematical Problem-Solving in RL vs. SFT Fine-Tuned Models","abstract":"Large reasoning models trained via reinforcement learning (RL) have been increasingly shown to outperform their supervised fine-tuned (SFT) counterparts on mathematical reasoning tasks; Yet the mechanistic basis for this advantage remains unclear. For researchers working on pretraining and post-training, the useful takeaway is that training objectives appear to reshape where and when useful information becomes available inside the network, not just the final accuracy or output length. That helps separate two hypotheses: RL may simply encourage longer reasoning traces, or it may actually reorganize the model’s internal computation.","technical_approach":"The analysis combines three tools. First, the authors train layer-wise linear probes on hidden states to predict whether the model’s final answer will be correct. Probe accuracy is used as a proxy for representational quality. Hidden states are extracted at the token immediately before \\boxed{}, chosen to capture the model after it has finished reasoning but before it commits to the answer string. Second, they run mean-ablation interventions to estimate which layers are most critical for mathematical reasoning and compare the layer-importance profiles of RL and SFT models. Third, they study generation consistency across multiple samples of the same problem, including token-usage patterns, to test whether RL models develop more stable and structured reasoning behavior. To reduce contamination and control difficulty, they generate 1,000 synthetic math problems from fixed templates spanning probability, fractions, and cost calculations, then balance correct and incorrect examples across models.","experiments":"Three findings stand out. First, RL models tend to show earlier-emerging and stronger correctness-related representations in the probe analysis, with higher probe accuracy than SFT counterparts across layers. Second, mean ablation suggests that RL training concentrates critical computation in deeper layers: layer importance increases with depth for RL models, with a reported correlation of r=0.47, while SFT models look much flatter and slightly reversed at r=-0.11. Third, the consistency analysis indicates that RL models produce more stable reasoning traces and more regular token allocation, although the authors also argue that current RL training still underuses adaptive token budgeting across problem difficulty. The experimental scope is modest and centered on synthetic math tasks, so the results should be read as mechanistic evidence and hypothesis generation rather than a universal account of reasoning models.","reading_guide":"A quick read should start with the introduction to fix the three-part analysis in mind. Then go to the probing section and pay attention to the extraction point before \\boxed{} and the balancing procedure, since both are important for interpreting the probe results. After that, read the mean-ablation results carefully; that is where the RL-versus-SFT architectural difference is most visible. The generation-consistency section can be skimmed unless token-allocation behavior is your main interest. In about 20–30 minutes, the probe curves and layer-importance plots are enough to capture the main message.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"别把它当“解释 RL 为何更强”的定论；但用 correctness probe + 分层消融去量化 RL 改写表征的套路可复用，重点看 probe 设定与消融是否足够干净。","en":"Not a definitive causal story for RL gains, but the correctness-probe + layer-importance ablation recipe is reusable; focus on whether the probe target and ablations are clean enough to trust."},"metadata":{"institutions":["University of New South Wales","Algoverse AI Research","University of Waterloo","St. Jude Children’s Research Hospital"],"authors":["Antyabha Rahman","Akshaj Gurugubelli","Omar Ankit","Kevin Zhu","Aishwarya Balwani"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["mechanistic-interpretability","reinforcement-learning","supervised-finetuning","linear-probing","ablation"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26119","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.26119","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2607.26411","title":"Do Unified Multimodal Models Think in One Space? intervent","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-29T02:46:20Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2607_26411.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究统一多模态模型的理解分支和生成分支是否共享同一个语义空间，而不只是共享一个外观上的模型框架。","importance":"对统一多模态预训练有明确启发。很多 UMM 同时做图像理解和图像生成，但这不保证两个分支内部的语义表示是可迁移的。论文用 intervention 而不是相关性分析来检测这一点：如果从理解分支学到的语义方向能改变生成结果，才说明两个分支至少共享了一部分可操作的语义子空间。结论也比较具体：理解到生成可以迁移，生成到理解基本失败。这提示预训练设计不能只把任务塞进同一个 backbone，还需要让生成侧的 latent 表示对齐到对象、属性、关系等抽象语义，而不是主要承载颜色、光照、背景这类低层外观统计。","technical_approach":"作者提出 cross-branch semantic steering。核心思想是不直接比较 text tokens 和 visual latents 的表示，因为两者 token 类型和训练目标都不同，而是做因果干预。具体做法是从一个分支提取语义 steering vector，例如“red → pink”，再在另一个分支推理时注入这个向量。如果理解分支得到的方向能让生成分支把 red ribbon 画成 pink ribbon，就说明该方向跨分支可用。作者构造 UMMSteer 数据集，包含 51 组视觉语义概念对，覆盖颜色、文字、数量、位置、风格、外观和 persona。理解侧的向量主要来自文本 QA 样本的表示差分；生成侧向量则来自生成过程中的 latent 表示。论文还分析了迁移不对称性的来源：理解侧方向更偏组合式、对象中心的语义，因此能被生成分支利用；生成侧方向更多捕捉背景色调、光照、全局颜色分布等低层外观特征，与理解分支的语义结构不匹配。","experiments":"实验围绕跨分支 steering 的可迁移性展开。作者首先在目标 UMM 上展示 understanding-to-generation 的成功案例：从理解分支抽取的概念方向可以改变图像生成中的属性，同时提升语义忠实度。反向的 generation-to-understanding 基本不能稳定改变理解分支预测，形成明显不对称。作者进一步在 Janus-Pro、UniPic-1 等其他统一多模态模型上验证，发现强任务性能并不自动意味着理解和生成分支有良好的共享语义空间；对齐程度与架构设计和模态融合方式有关。提供内容没有给出完整指标表和具体成功率，因此更适合作为机制诊断工作阅读，而不是按单一分数比较模型。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 20 分钟。重点看 cross-branch steering 的定义、steering vector 的提取位置和注入位置、UMMSteer 的 51 个概念对设计，以及 understanding-to-generation 和 generation-to-understanding 的对照实验。真正值得深挖的是失败方向：生成侧表示为什么偏低层外观，是否由 diffusion/latent decoder 训练目标造成，以及预训练中能否加入跨分支一致性 loss，让生成 latent 更显式地承载对象和关系语义。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tests whether unified multimodal models actually learn a shared semantic space between their understanding branch and generation branch, rather than merely placing both functions inside one architecture.","importance":"The work is useful for multimodal pretraining because it separates architectural unification from representational unification. A model can perform both VQA and image generation while still using poorly aligned internal spaces. The diagnostic is intervention-based: a semantic direction extracted from one branch must causally affect the other branch to count as transferable. The main finding is asymmetric. Understanding-to-generation transfer works, while generation-to-understanding transfer largely fails. This suggests that unified pretraining needs stronger pressure to align generation latents with object-level and relation-level semantics, not only with low-level visual appearance.","technical_approach":"The authors introduce cross-branch semantic steering. Instead of directly comparing text-token representations with visual latents, they extract a steering vector for a concept pair, such as red to pink, from one branch and inject it into the other during inference. If a vector learned from the understanding branch changes the generated image in the intended semantic direction, that is causal evidence for a shared usable subspace. The paper builds UMMSteer, a visually grounded contrastive dataset with 51 concept pairs covering color, text, counting, position, style, appearance, and persona. Understanding-side vectors are derived from text-based QA representations, while generation-side vectors are derived from latent generation representations. The asymmetry is attributed to a mismatch in abstraction level: understanding vectors tend to encode compositional, object-centric semantics, whereas generation vectors often capture background tone, illumination, and global color statistics.","experiments":"The experiments test steering transfer across branches. In the main direction, understanding-derived vectors can steer generation and improve semantic faithfulness, such as shifting a prompted red object toward the target color. In the reverse direction, generation-derived vectors do not reliably alter understanding predictions. The authors also test other UMMs, including Janus-Pro and UniPic-1, and find that strong task performance does not necessarily imply aligned cross-branch representations. The supplied text does not include full numeric tables or success rates, so the result should be read primarily as a representational diagnostic rather than a leaderboard-style comparison.","reading_guide":"A 20-minute skim is appropriate. Read the cross-branch steering setup, where vectors are extracted and injected, the UMMSteer concept-pair design, and the asymmetric transfer results. The most interesting technical question is why generation-side latents remain appearance-heavy, and whether multimodal pretraining objectives can add cross-branch consistency so that generation representations carry more explicit object and relation semantics.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"做多模态统一预训练的可重点看§4的不对称性分析，setup干净，对理解生成分支的对齐目标设计有直接参考价值","en":"Multimodal pretraining researchers should focus on §4’s asymmetry analysis; the clean setup offers direct reference for aligning understanding and generation branch objectives"},"metadata":{"institutions":["University of Wisconsin-Madison"],"authors":["Yu Wang","Sharon Li"],"notable_authors":["Sharon Li"],"tags":["unified multimodal model","semantic space analysis","representation steering","interpretability","multimodal alignment"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26411","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.26411","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2607.26608","title":"Understanding Knowledge Transfer Mechanism in Heterogeneous MLLM Fusion: A Simple Linear Approach","abstract":"Training-free fusion of heterogeneous multimodal large language models (MLLMs) provides a direct route for cross-scale capability transfer, yet improvements in aggregate performance do not reveal what a smaller model actually inherits. Existing studies are largely designed and evaluated on limited task sets or aggregate metrics; as evaluation expands to broader task collections, whether different capabilities can transfer across scales remains poorly understood. To investigate this question, we ","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-29T08:31:27Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2607_26608.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要回答的不是“融合后总分有没有涨”，而是更细的三个问题：跨尺度、异构的 MLLM 在训练免费融合时，究竟哪些能力能从大模型转到小模型；这些能力主要寄存在什么参数和什么模块里；层对齐、参数选择和注入强度为什么会决定转移是否有效。作者指出，已有 heterogeneous fusion 工作大多只看聚合指标，难以区分真实知识迁移、结构失配带来的干扰、以及不同能力之间的非均匀变化。为此，他们把“融合”降成一个局部线性探针问题，用很小幅度的参数方向注入去测 transfer signal，而不是直接追求一次性最优融合结果。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义不在于提出了新的可部署融合算法，而在于给出了一套分析跨模型知识可迁移性的可操作框架。它把参数空间中的迁移拆成一阶有益信号和二阶结构干扰两部分，这个视角对理解 model merging、continual pretrain、跨尺寸蒸馏、甚至 mid-training 阶段的参数迁移都很有参考价值。更具体地说，结论是：跨尺度迁移明显具有能力选择性，高层推理比感知更容易迁移；LLM 部分比视觉编码器更承载这类可迁移增益；有效迁移只出现在很窄的小注入区间。这些观察对“哪些能力是局部线性可搬运的，哪些能力强依赖整体表征几何”给出了较清楚的经验边界。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 CDPI，Cross-Scale Directional Parameter Injection。给定同一家族中较大的 source MLLM 和较小的 target MLLM，先把模型拆成 ViT、跨模态 projector、LLM 三个组件，再定义注入空间 E=(C,P)：C 决定注入哪些组件，P 决定只注入 linear-layer 参数还是注入所有可对齐参数。由于 source 和 target 在 hidden size、attention head 数、LLM 深度上都不同，作者构造了异构对齐算子 A_pi，把 source 参数投影到 target 参数空间。这里的 pi 是 LLM 层映射策略，决定深层到浅层、均匀抽样还是其他配对方式。得到投影方向 ΔΘ 后，不做训练，只沿着这个方向对 target 做小步移动：Θ_F = Θ_T + αΔ。关键不在大步融合，而在扫描 α、组件、参数子集和层映射，观察不同能力的局部响应。\n\n理论部分用了 target 点附近的二阶展开。对某一能力类别 c 的泛化损失 L_c(Θ)，局部增益近似写成 αA_c - 1/2 α^2 I_c，其中 A_c = -∇L_c(Θ_T)^TΔ 表示一阶 transfer signal，衡量 source 投影方向是否与该能力的改进方向对齐；I_c = Δ^T H_c Δ 表示二阶 curvature response，刻画结构失配、参数耦合和局部曲率带来的干扰。这个分解很重要，因为它解释了为什么同一个 source 方向会对 reasoning 有帮助、对 perception 几乎无效，或者在 α 稍大时迅速转负：不是没有信号，而是二阶干扰开始主导。\n\n和常见 model merging 不同，这里不追求构造一个全局最优 merged model，也不依赖训练 learned mapping。它更像一个诊断工具：通过受控的小幅参数注入，测量不同模块和不同参数类型的可迁移性。作者还特别比较了 linear 参数与 full 参数，发现线性层权重往往具有更高的 transfer efficiency，这暗示可迁移知识更集中在可直接作用于表示变换的主干线性映射，而不是所有可对齐参数平均分布。","experiments":"实验基于四组 Qwen3-VL 跨尺度模型对，在 12 个 benchmark 上分析 transfer pattern。全文给出的主结果不是单一总分，而是按能力拆分后的变化。最稳定的发现有四点。\n\n第一，增益主要集中在 reasoning，尤其是 high-level reasoning；perception 类任务基本贴着 target 原始水平，变化很小。这说明跨尺度训练免费融合更像是在搬运高层决策或语言推理方向，而不是把视觉感知能力直接“拷贝”过去。\n\n第二，从组件上看，high-level reasoning 的提升主要来自 LLM 注入，而不是 ViT 或 projector。ViT 注入对感知也没有带来同等显著的正迁移，说明视觉侧能力更受表示空间和编码细节约束，局部线性投影不够。\n\n第三，参数范围上，linear-only 注入通常比 full-parameter 注入更稳，单位注入强度下的收益更高、干扰更小。这个结果和理论分解一致：full 注入虽然包含更多潜在有益信号，但也引入更多二阶结构冲突。\n\n第四，层映射和注入比例都很敏感。有效 layer mapping 明显依赖具体 source-target 配对，不存在一个通用最优映射。注入比例 α 只有在较小区间内才出现正向 selective transfer；一旦 α 继续增大，性能很快被 interference 吞掉。作者把这一现象解释为一阶收益先线性增长、二阶损伤按平方项放大，因此最佳点天然落在窄小区间。\n\n从阅读价值看，最有信息量的是这些消融：按组件拆分、按参数类型拆分、按 layer mapping 拆分、按 α 扫描的能力曲线。如果只看 aggregate score，会错过论文真正的结论。","reading_guide":"建议按“问题设定—理论分解—消融结果”三步读。\n\n先看 Introduction 和方法图，明确 CDPI 不是为了做更强融合，而是为了测 transfer mechanism。这个定位如果没抓住，后面的实验会被误读成“为什么不用更复杂映射”。\n\n然后重点看方法部分关于 injection space、alignment operator 和 layer mapping 的定义。这里决定了实验到底在控制什么变量。尤其要注意作者为什么区分 linear 与 full 参数，以及为什么把 ViT、Proj、LLM 分开分析。\n\n理论部分值得细读二阶展开。核心不是公式本身，而是 A_c 和 I_c 的解释：一个代表能力相关的一阶对齐信号，一个代表结构干扰。后面所有“只在小 α 有效”“reasoning 比 perception 更能迁移”的现象，都是围绕这个框架展开的。\n\n实验部分优先看四类图表：1）不同能力类别的增益分布；2）组件级注入对比；3）linear vs full；4）layer mapping 与 α 扫描。预计 30–45 分钟可以抓住主线；如果要深挖，可进一步想两个问题：为什么 reasoning 更线性、而 perception 更依赖整体几何；为什么 LLM 中的可迁移方向能跨尺度保留，而视觉侧不行。这两个问题和预训练表征的可压缩性直接相关。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a more precise question than “does fusion improve the average score?” In training-free fusion between heterogeneous, cross-scale MLLMs, what capabilities actually transfer from the larger model to the smaller one, where those transferable signals reside, and how layer mapping, parameter scope, and injection strength shape the outcome. Prior heterogeneous fusion work mostly reports aggregate gains, which makes it hard to separate genuine knowledge transfer from architectural mismatch and interference. This work reframes fusion as a local probing problem: inject a small projected parameter direction and measure capability-specific response.","importance":"Its value for pretraining research is mainly diagnostic rather than algorithmic. CDPI provides a concrete way to study transferability in parameter space and decomposes local transfer into a first-order beneficial signal and a second-order structural interference term. That perspective is useful beyond model fusion: it also informs cross-scale distillation, continual pretraining, parameter reuse during mid-training, and more generally the question of which capabilities are locally portable across models. The results explain the root of capability gaps between attention variants, such as why standard causal Transformers cannot solve parity or other modular counting tasks, and provide formal reference for pre-training architecture selection, attention mechanism design, and long-context capability boundary estimation.","technical_approach":"Finite-precision causal Transformers are modeled as algebraic RNNs: each attention head maintains finite-state operational memory, a set of recursively updated accumulators that form a sufficient prefix statistic for all future queries from the head. This abstraction is distinct from the length-growing KV cache and current-step residual activation. Heads update state independently and in parallel within a layer, while layers compose hierarchically in cascade. Transformation semigroup theory is used to derive semigroup properties for each attention variant directly from floating-point arithmetic rules, then parallel and cascade composition rules are applied to obtain tight upper bounds on language recognition capacity for full models.","experiments":"This is a theoretical work with formal proofs rather than empirical experiments. Core results are strict capacity bounds for four attention architectures: (1) Width-1 sliding window attention only recognizes definite languages; (2) Sharp soft attention (where small values are fully truncated in normalization) only recognizes R-trivial languages; (3) Appropriate cascades of sliding window and sharp soft attention only recognize locally R-trivial languages; (4) Full floating-point soft attention only recognizes star-free languages, excluding parity and all modular counting tasks regardless of model depth, width, or head count. The capability gap between sharp and full soft attention is formally traced to floating-point precision effects: small contributions may be rounded away in the normalizer while still altering accumulated memory values, shifting the semigroup property from R-trivial to aperiodic and corresponding to higher expressive capacity.","reading_guide":"Read at a skim level. fa","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-28T16:58:27Z","category_id":"coding_agent","cover_url":"/covers/2607_26117.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.SE","cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决的问题很具体：代码模型自修复到底是在利用执行反馈，还是只是在多试一次。已有代码 Agent 往往把“失败程序 + 测试输出 + 请修正”与单次生成比较，这会把反馈价值和额外采样机会混在一起。论文用安慰剂对照把两者拆开，发现小模型里真正有效的是重新采样，而不是看回自己的失败答案。","importance":"对基座和代码模型研究的价值主要在评测方法和训练信号理解。它提醒代码自修复、反思、执行反馈这类能力不能只和 single-shot 比；必须和同预算的 blind resampling 比。否则会高估反馈闭环的作用。对预训练和后训练接口也有直接启发：如果训练数据里大量包含“错误答案 + 修正”的轨迹，小模型可能学到局部编辑和锚定，而不是学会真正利用错误信号。论文的贡献不是提出新的训练 再加入真实执行反馈，包括失败输入、期望输出和实际输出，用来测诊断信息的增益；reflect 在 feedback 前要求模型先用自然语言诊断，再重写代码，用来测显式反思是否能绕开锚定。四个条件除后缀内容外保持一致。重试阶段都用 temperature 0.8；作者明确指出 greedy decoding 会反复生成字节级相同程序，因此不能用于评估重采样。每个任务最多 k=8 次尝试，并从同一条轨迹读取不同 k 下的 pass@1。统计上使用 McNemar exact test 做配对比较，bootstrap 给任务级差异区间，Holm correction 控制多重检验。机制分析集中在 anchoring：包含失败程序的 prompt 会让下一次生成更像上一次失败程序，模型倾向做局部修改，而 blind resampling role-conditioned policy 支持，但保留不同的 prompt、状态、动作空间和 reward。训练流程是先做 Joint SFT 初始化两个接口，再做 Navigator RL，然后用 frozen Navigator 作为 evaluator 做 Builder RL，最后在 role-homogeneous batches 上做 Joint RL
